### 🆕 OPEN 2026-08-03 - Playtest feedback (District Zero Session 3, "super positive, for the most part")
Xero's notes from tonight, plus two recorder dumps (2h08m main session + a short session-management one). Diagnosed one cross-cutting finding from the dump before triaging (see item 2/5 below). Numbering matches Xero's own list.

- **[1] Multi-anchor point drawing tool for the map, both players and GMs.** New feature - freehand/multi-point drawing/annotation on the campaign map, distinct from the existing MEASURE and ROUTE tools (both single-purpose, not general drawing). Routed to HP as backlog, needs real scoping before estimating.
- **[2 + 5] NPC/pin reveal control "should be controlled by the GM" (confirms current design intent) but required a lot of toggling; concretely had trouble showing/hiding Melissa & Billy at the gate.** **Diagnosed from the recorder dump:** the whole realtime connection dropped and reconnected 3 separate times during the session (22:37:18, 22:38:25-36, 22:40:54, 23:05:41 UTC) - EVERY table-page channel (table/members/campaign/campaign_npcs/reveals/initiative/community_members/advantages/init-scene-tags) hit CHANNEL_ERROR then TIMED_OUT simultaneously each time, a whole-socket blip, not a per-feature bug. The `reveals_` channel was one of the 9 caught in each blip. Plausible contributing cause for the toggling friction: a client mid-reconnect wouldn't receive a reveal broadcast, requiring the GM to retry until that client's own catch-up/reconnect logic caught it back up. Not 100% pinned to these exact clicks (nearest logged clicks in the blip windows were an NPC Edit/Save, not a dedicated Hide/Reveal click) but the reconnect pattern itself is a real, confirmed reliability finding worth a look regardless of whether it's the full explanation. Routed to Puffer to investigate the reconnect frequency/catch-up robustness.
- **[3] Visual dice roller for d3/d4/d6/d8/d10/d12/d20/d100.** New UI/polish feature - animated/visual die instead of (or alongside) the math-only roll resolution. Routed to HP as backlog.
- **[6] [SHIPPED v1 `eef41270` 2026-08-03, HP] Wallet field on character sheets for District Zero characters - tracks BB's (Bullets & Batteries), the setting's actual currency.** District-Zero-only per Xero (2026-08-03). v1 SHIPPED: `CharacterCard.tsx` wallet gated on a new `setting` prop (`=== 'district_zero'`, threaded from the table page's `campaign.setting`); two separate denominations in `character.data.wallet` `{bullets,batteries}` + derived "N BB's" total; pip UI mirrors the Ammo (click-to-set) + Clips (-/+ growing pip) chrome, click-to-set capped at 20 to avoid accidental reset on large hoards; persists via `updateCharacterDataField`. FOLLOW-UPS (not done): (a) hook into the trade/vendor/barter flow so costs deduct BB's automatically (the reason the two denominations + total were kept as a real shape, not one opaque number); (b) the `/character-sheet` popout route also renders CharacterCard but doesn't fetch `setting`, so the wallet won't show there until threaded (same pattern as the npc-sheet setting plumbing) - low priority, table sheet is the primary surface. Canon confirmed via sourcebook excerpt: "no fixed currency... deals are struck through barter and haggling, but if the city requires payment... it takes it in the form of bullets or batteries... each bullet and each battery count as one credit" - a real spendable currency ("things cost BB's"), not a flavor counter. v1 scope routed to HP: a manual counter on the character sheet, gated to `campaign.setting === 'district_zero'`. Flagged that it may eventually need to hook into the existing trade/barter surface (vendor NPCs, the Trade negotiation modal) rather than staying a standalone number, once that's worth the lift.
- **[7] Two Gut Instinct rolls canceled out the first Whisper modal - modals need to be persistent until the GM saves/cancels/exits/sends.** Real modal-state bug: a newer modal (Gut Instinct) is closing/clobbering an in-progress different modal (Whisper) instead of stacking or blocking. Routed to HP.
- **[8] Need a scavenging merchant NPC (Finley) in the Farmer's Market - add to the District Zero sourcebook.** Content-authoring task (setting sourcebook + in-campaign NPC), not code - Xero's creative call on Finley's characterization; happy to help mechanically (insert the NPC row) once written.
- **[9] NPC card should be consistent regardless of entry point - via a map pin OR via the NPC roster, it should look the same and have First Impression/Recruit/etc.** Real UI consistency bug - at least two different NPC card renderings depending on how you got there, with different feature sets. - **[10] Add opposed checks - currently a placeholder.** Known, already-flagged gap (a real core mechanic, not a quick fix). Stays in the backlog, not newly discovered tonight.

### ✅ Shipped 2026-08-03 - world_npcs moderation RLS - Thrivers couldn't approve others' submissions
Found while assessing HP's silent-write-failure sweep (`tasks/silent-write-failure-rls-subset-2026-08-02.md`). A Thriver could approve/reject their OWN world_npc submission fine but not another user's - the UPDATE silently matched 0 rows, and the pending queue's LIST was invisible for others' submissions too (no Thriver SELECT policy on `world_npcs` at all - every sibling moderation table already has one). Root cause: an inline correlated subquery on `profiles` inside the UPDATE policy instead of the proven `is_thriver()` helper every sibling table uses - same fragile shape as the campaign_members incident above, different failure signature (silent 0-row match, not recursion). Fixed both the UPDATE and the missing SELECT policy, verified live (own-row baseline confirmed working, other-user case confirmed broken then confirmed fixed). Also cleared `stories/new:196` (GM auto-join) as NOT an active bug - simulated the exact live sequence under the current policy, succeeds cleanly. `sql/fix-world-npcs-moderation-rls-2026-08-03.sql`.

### ✅ Shipped 2026-08-03 - campaign_members <-> characters RLS recursion, hit live mid-playtest
Xero reported: clicking ASSIGN to give Pesky Larue her existing "Cruz Zwick" character in District Zero silently did nothing, tried a few times. Root cause: my own 2026-08-01 fix (character-ownership check on `campaign_members` UPDATE) queries `characters`, whose own SELECT policy queries back into `campaign_members` - a genuine cross-table RLS cycle, raising `"infinite recursion detected in policy"` on any assign-EXISTING-character action (silently swallowed by the client's `if (!error) {}` pattern, no message ever shown). Only surfaces on reusing an already-existing character across a new campaign - first-time joins create a fresh character via a different path, so this sat live for 2 days unnoticed. Fixed by routing the ownership check through a new `SECURITY DEFINER` helper (`user_owns_character`), same pattern as `is_campaign_member()`/`is_thriver()` - bypasses RLS internally, breaks the cycle. Verified live: the exact failing UPDATE now succeeds. `sql/fix-campaign-members-characters-rls-recursion-2026-08-03.sql`, commit `f763847b`. Lesson logged in `tasks/lessons.md` - also flags the wider "no error surfaced on write failure" pattern as worth a sweep (routed to HP separately).

### ✅ Shipped 2026-08-02 - portrait-bank confidentiality, option (b) - private portraits now RLS-enforced
Xero's decision via Comms (`tasks/COMMS.md`): separate `portrait-bank-private` bucket (public:false, own-uid RLS) for private character portraits; shared public bank stays on plain `getPublicUrl()` forever - zero redesign/expiry risk for the majority case. Root cause: `portrait-bank` bucket was `public:true`, so `private/<uid>/...` objects were fully readable by anyone with/guessing the URL regardless of any RLS policy (write-side closed 2026-08-01, this is the read-side follow-up).
- Verified live FIRST that Supabase accepts a 10-year `createSignedUrl` expiry with no cap (a maintainer demonstrated exactly this) before committing to the design - avoided repeating the same "build on an unverified assumption" mistake that hit option (a) earlier in this same thread.
- New bucket + own-uid SELECT/INSERT/DELETE RLS live. `uploadPrivatePortrait()` (`lib/data/portrait-bank.ts`) now uploads there and signs once at upload time (10yr expiry), storing that URL exactly like `getPublicUrl()`'s result was stored - **zero consumer changes needed** (`PortraitBankPicker`, NPC roster, random-pick all just render whatever URL is in the DB).
- Investigated `app/tools/token-creator/page.tsx`'s own `isPrivate` checkbox - turned out to be a SEPARATE, lower-severity issue (metadata-only pool-exclusion flag, storage path never uses the `private/` convention at all) rather than a duplicate of this bug. Flagged separately via spawn_task rather than folded into this fix.
- Migrated the 4 existing private-portrait rows/objects (1 user): CLI cross-bucket move/copy unsupported, so download+reupload via `supabase storage cp`, re-signed URLs, updated DB rows, deleted old objects, dropped the 2 now-dead policies on the old bucket. Verified live at every step: old public URL now 400s, new bucket rejects unsigned access entirely ("Bucket not found" - not a public bucket), new signed URL confirmed serving the correct image (200, correct bytes).
tsc/font/role/em-dash/arch/937 tests green, commit `15a8ab2d`, fast-forwarded straight to `main`. **Not yet click-tested:** the in-app upload flow as a logged-in Thriver - no test credentials available this session. The storage-layer mechanism it depends on is verified live above; worth a 2-minute manual pass next time a Thriver's in the Create Tokens tool.

### ✅ Shipped 2026-08-02 - Account-deletion anonymize - COMPLETE, schema + UI, top-level AND reply tables
Xero's decision via Comms: build the "anonymized rather than deleted" promise (`app/account/page.tsx`) for real instead of correcting the copy. Full end-to-end, both halves, all 7 tables:
- **Schema (Puffer Fish):** `author_user_id` nullable + `ON DELETE CASCADE` -> `SET NULL` on `forum_threads`/`war_stories`/`lfg_posts`/`whispers` (`cecc19c7`), then on the 3 reply tables `forum_replies`/`lfg_post_replies`/`war_story_replies` (`944b9e2b`) once HP found those carry their OWN separate author FK (independent of `thread_id`/`post_id`) that was still `CASCADE` - a REPLIER deleting their account was still hard-deleting their reply out of someone else's surviving thread, the same promise violation one layer down. All 7 now match the existing `modules.author_user_id` pattern. Live SQL applied + verified in `information_schema` each time, `database.types.ts` regenerated twice.
- **UI (Hunt & Peck):** top-level tables (`a750f516`) then the reply tables (`4404dcbd`). Along the way caught a real secondary bug: a null in a PostgREST `.in('id', authorIds)` returns HTTP 400 and fails the WHOLE query, not a graceful per-row degrade - null-filtered before every `.in()` across forums/forums2/forums-[id]/war-stories/lfg/whispers(`MapView.tsx`)/settings/`InlineRepliesPanel.tsx`, display distinguishes null (deleted -> **'Anonymous'**) from an unresolved id ('Unknown'), DM-link/avatar-color suppressed for null authors, interfaces made honest (`string | null`) so tsc surfaced every secondary site. `app/moderate` was already `.filter(Boolean)`-safe, left as-is.
tsc/arch/font/role/em-dash/937 tests green throughout on both sides. Nothing further owed.

### ✅ Shipped 2026-08-02 - campaign_npcs.hidden_from_players GM-only (hub/spoke first catch)
Found via Hunt & Peck's NPC<->pin feature hand-off (first real use of the new hub/spoke model, see `tasks/HUB-LIVE.md`) - HP correctly flagged rather than touched: "Campaign members update campaign_npcs" had no column restriction, so any campaign member (not just GM) could write `hidden_from_players` - directly undoing the 2026-08-01 fog-of-war SELECT-side fix, since a player could just flip the flag off to reveal a hidden NPC to themselves. Scoped narrowly (BEFORE UPDATE trigger clamps only `hidden_from_players` to OLD for non-GM) since the table also holds legitimate any-member-writable combat-live-state fields (wp/rp/death_countdown/incap_rounds/inventory/skills) that real gameplay code writes from non-GM sessions - verified every `updateCampaignNpc`/raw `campaign_npcs.update` call site first, only `NpcRoster.tsx`'s GM-only `quickReveal()` ever touches this column. Live SQL applied + verified.

### ✅ Shipped 2026-08-01 - Audit wave 3 (Puffer, 10 fixes, `e56fb050`..`a1f0a96d`)
Xero: "keep going, don't want to stop till your audit/fixes are complete." Wave 2's original scope was exhausted, so wave 3 = 6 parallel agents into genuinely untouched territory: DM/messages system, GM-kit deep pass, storage/object upload RLS, `/moderate` admin access control, forum/campfire beyond the earlier rate-limit fix, account/profile/auth flows. Two independent agents (the `/moderate` pass and the campfire pass) converged on the identical bug shape from different angles - strong cross-validation. 10 fixes (5 CRITICAL, 2 HIGH, 3 MEDIUM).
- [x] **[CRITICAL, `e56fb050`] profiles self-write let a suspended user un-suspend themselves and self-grant TheTableau director** - no WITH CHECK beyond row ownership on 4 trust-tier columns (suspended/suspended_until/suspended_reason/tableau_role). New BEFORE UPDATE trigger, same shape as the existing `role` self-promotion guard.
- [x] **[CRITICAL, `3db02a9d`] Moderation self-approval recurs on 5 more tables** - world_npcs (no clamp at all), modules (INSERT+UPDATE), forum_threads/lfg_posts/war_stories (UPDATE only, INSERT already correctly gated). 9 new triggers; modules' fix recomputes from `visibility` (matching the app's own existing rule) rather than a blanket clamp, so "flip a private module to listed" still correctly re-enters review.
- [x] **[CRITICAL, `3269f420`] portrait-bank: any user could upload into another user's private/ folder** - write-side only; narrowed the bucket-wide unscoped INSERT policy to exclude `private/` paths. **Read-side NOT fixed - see below, needs Xero's input.**
- [x] **[HIGH, `20025ad1`] forum_replies/war_story_replies INSERT allowed cross-campaign content injection** - read was campaign-scoped, write wasn't. Added the matching EXISTS clause to both INSERT policies.
- [x] **[MEDIUM, `c2bb7dd9`] DM system had no suspension or rate-limit gating** - the only user-content INSERT policy in the schema missing both checks. Added, 120/hour.
- [x] **[MEDIUM, `a1f0a96d`] GM-kit export had no server-side GM check + 4 of 5 queries silently truncated at 1000 rows** - added explicit GM check + routed pins/npcs/scenes/notes through the existing `fetchAllRows` pagination helper (scene_tokens already had this).
- [x] **[process, `a1f0a96d`+`f1dc1142`] Fixed a stale arch baseline + captured the lesson** - `check-arch.mjs --save` only ever lowers a LOC ceiling (Math.min); a genuine growth needs `--save --force`. Bit wave 2's CAS fix silently (looked saved, wasn't), caught while re-baselining for the GM-kit fix.

**NOT fixed - both need Xero's input before proceeding, not more automatic fixing:**
- **portrait-bank read-side confidentiality.** The bucket itself is `public: true` - Supabase Storage serves every object via an unauthenticated CDN URL with NO RLS evaluation for reads, so "private" character portraits are still fully readable by anyone with/guessing the URL. Real fix is either (a) flip the bucket private and rework every `getPublicUrl()` consumer to signed URLs (breaks image loading everywhere - public AND private - until every consumer is updated), or (b) migrate private uploads to a genuinely separate private bucket (a live data migration for already-uploaded files). Either way this touches real users' already-uploaded images and needs browser verification before shipping - too risky to rush blind into an already-large batch. **Recommend:** scope this as its own focused session with 2-browser verification, same rigor as a tactical-map fix.
- **Account self-deletion cascades into OTHER users' content, contradicting the app's own "anonymized rather than deleted" promise.** `app/account/page.tsx` copy says entangled content (forum posts, etc.) gets anonymized on deletion; the FKs say otherwise - `forum_threads`/`war_stories`/`lfg_posts`/`whispers` are `author_user_id ON DELETE CASCADE`, and their reply tables cascade a second level off the PARENT (not the author), so self-deleting hard-deletes a popular thread's replies from every OTHER user who replied to it, with zero warning to them. `modules.author_user_id` is correctly `ON DELETE SET NULL` (anonymize) - that pattern was just never extended to the other 4 tables. Fixing this needs a product decision, not just a schema patch: either build the anonymize behavior for real (nullable author_user_id + "Anonymous" UI fallback across 4 more content types), or update the account-deletion copy to match what actually happens. **This is Xero's call, not mine to make silently** - it's a promise made to users about their data.
- [x] **[CRITICAL, `551b1bd9`] campaign_invitations UPDATE let any user bypass private-campaign membership + spam notifications** - no column-pinning on UPDATE let an attacker retarget campaign_id/sender/recipient. BEFORE UPDATE trigger now pins all three.
- [x] **[CRITICAL, `55c0c317`] map_pins UPDATE let any user self-approve a fake global World Event CMod** - moderation clamp only fired on INSERT. BEFORE UPDATE trigger added, mirrors INSERT clamp, also closes an INSERT-time self-buff variant.
- [x] **[CRITICAL, `81dec574`] world_communities UPDATE let the publishing GM/leader self-approve** - no trigger re-asserted moderation_status on UPDATE. BEFORE UPDATE trigger pins moderation_status/approved_by/approved_at to Thrivers only.
- [x] **[CRITICAL, `e4844cbd`] Classic character editor silently wiped inventory/progression/wounds/CDP on every save** - buildCharacter() wrote a blank-template object wholesale. New mergeWizardEdit() + saveWizardCharacterEdit() merge only wizard-owned fields onto a fresh read. 3 new unit tests.
- [x] **[HIGH, `4334a839`] Module pregen clone deterministically failed on every import** - author_id:null could never satisfy pregen_library_insert's RLS. New clone_module_pregens_into_campaign SECURITY DEFINER RPC. database.types.ts regenerated (was stale since before wave 1's new RPCs).
- [x] **[HIGH, `a3aa3cdd`] characters SELECT let any campaign co-member read ALL of a peer's characters** - peer-read policy joined on user_id instead of character_id like its GM-read sibling. Fixed to match.
- [x] **[HIGH, `b189a4a1`] Alt+right-click door/window toggle used the pre-2026-05-31 no-op path** - a second entry point for the same action never got the May fix. Now routes through toggleWallSegmentDoor() like the plain-click path. Flagged for E2E lane: extend `e2e/wall-segment-door-cross-client.spec.ts`.
- [x] **[types, `5fb95268`] Pre-existing tsc break from wave 1's Insight-Trade fix (`e4759b1a`)** - PostgrestFilterBuilder isn't structurally a Promise; wrapped in Promise.resolve().
- [x] **[docs, `bbc612f8`] Closed stale line-461 window/LOS item** - superseded by the already-shipped 2026-05-31 fix; see the alt+right-click finding above for what's actually still open there.
- [x] **[CRITICAL, `fcf3b3d0`] Fog-of-war and hidden-NPC data enforced client-side only** - `campaign_npcs` and `scene_tokens` each had a wide-open duplicate SELECT policy coexisting with a correctly-scoped one (Postgres ORs PERMISSIVE policies together); the wide one always won. Full hidden-NPC stat blocks and every hidden token's exact position/HP/loot were readable by any campaign member via a direct query regardless of the GM's hide toggle. Dropped the duplicates; scene_tokens now gates on `is_visible` (own/co-controlled tokens always visible). `tactical_scenes.walls` "undiscovered door" concern checked and found NOT a bug - no secrecy flag exists in the wall-segment schema, nothing to leak.
- [x] **[HIGH, `eac356dd`] Schema-wide sweep for the same duplicate-policy pattern found 2 more live bugs** - `forum_threads`/`lfg_posts`/`war_stories`/`whispers` each had an old INSERT policy missing the suspension check + rate limit a newer sibling policy enforced (suspended users could still post everywhere; rate limits were fully bypassable). `npc_relationships` had a policy named "read own revealed relationships" whose USING clause never actually checked `revealed`, OR'd with an unconditional any-member policy - narrative reveal-gating was decorative. All fixed; one policy per table now.
- [x] **[CRITICAL, `f3e711fc`] Any campaign member could reassign any community member's labor role or force-kick them** - `community_members_update` had no WITH CHECK at all (USING reused); any member, not just GM, could rewrite ANY other member's role/status/left_at. The role `<select>` in `CampaignCommunity.tsx` had no `isGM` gate either - directly clickable in the UI by any player, no devtools needed. RLS now GM-only with a real WITH CHECK; UI shows a read-only label for non-GMs. Resolved the `character_states` UPDATE ambiguity too - checked `tasks/finding-characters-rls-cross-user-writes-2026-05-24.md` and confirmed that one's wide grant is a documented, already-accepted tradeoff (not touched).
- [x] **[MEDIUM, `af033196`] Module hard-delete bypass + module_subscriptions over-exposure closed** - `archiveModule()`'s subscriber-count guard was client-side only; added a BEFORE DELETE trigger backstop mirroring its own logic. `module_subscriptions_read` reverted from any-authenticated-user back to GM/member/author/Thriver; added `get_campaign_module_cover()` RPC (returns just the cover image, nothing else) for the one legitimate pre-membership need that motivated the original over-grant.
- [x] **[LOW, `25445f59`] Swept the whole schema for dead capital-case 'Thriver' RLS literals** - `profiles.role` is auto-lowercased, so `role = 'Thriver'` never matches. Found 5 policies across 4 tables (campaign_pins, map_pins x3, world_npcs, user_events); 3 were pure redundancy (map_pins has a working `is_thriver()` bypass elsewhere) but campaign_pins/world_npcs/user_events had NO other Thriver path - those moderation/read-all features were silently inert. All fixed to `lower(role) = 'thriver'`; verified zero remaining live.
- [x] **[MEDIUM, `22ecf9bb`] Stale-client-state save races on session notes, progression log, weapons, inventory** - 5 write sites in `app/character-sheet/page.tsx` + `CharacterCard.tsx` did `{...localSnapshot, field}` and wrote the whole `data` blob, risking silently reverting a concurrent change from another tab/session. All 5 routed through the existing `updateCharacterDataField` (fresh read + merge) helper. Arch ratchet improved (5 fewer raw `.from()` calls).
- [x] **[MEDIUM, `f11e032d`] GM multi-tab wall/fog persist race** - `scheduleWallsPersist`/`scheduleFogPersist` did a full-array/object replace on every debounced write; two GM-privileged sessions editing within the debounce window could silently clobber each other. New `updateSceneColumnCAS()` does the write with `.eq(column, expected)` - atomic compare-and-set, not a separate read-then-write. Lost-race writes are dropped (not silently applied) with a canvas notice on the walls path. Flagged for E2E lane: verifying true concurrent-session behavior needs real multi-client testing.

### 🐛 SESSION FIXES 2026-08-02 (HP)
- [x] **[SHIPPED - observability, Puffer-flagged] character-assignment writes failed silently.** `app/stories/[id]/page.tsx` - all three assign paths (`handleAssignCharacter` + the two pregen handlers `handleSelectPregen`/`handleSelectLibraryPregen`) did `if (!error) { ...update state... }` with NO else branch, so an RLS/constraint/trigger failure on that write was invisible: no message, no `console.error`, looked exactly like "the button did nothing" (this is what masked the 2026-08-03 campaign_members RLS-recursion bug and cost real debug time). Fixed: shared `assignError` state surfaced in-page below the Assign row (red inline box, `role="alert"`, NOT a browser dialog per `feedback_no_browser_dialogs`) + a `console.error` on each failure path; cleared on retry. **FOLLOW-UP (noted, not done):** a broader observability sweep for the same silent-failure shape (`if (!error)`-only / swallowed write errors) across `app/` + `components/` - the exact-string `if (!error)` count is only ~3, but the pattern has many variants (`if (!err)`, un-checked `const { error }`, etc.); a real sweep is its own focused pass, worth doing but bigger than this fix.
- [~] **[IN PROGRESS - observability sweep, HP] silent write-failure catalog (~55 user-facing sites).** A cataloging subagent (2026-08-02) swept `app/`+`components/` for Supabase writes whose error is swallowed (captured-never-used / `if(!error)`-no-else / result discarded) with zero surfacing. ROOT CAUSE: the codebase's established guard idiom (`.select()`+rowcount / `reportSupabaseError(err, ctx)` from `lib/supabase-errors.ts`) is applied INCONSISTENTLY - in many files a sibling handler on the same table checks+alerts while the flagged one doesn't (characters delete-vs-duplicate, stories remove-vs-leave, moderate NPC-vs-everything-else). Most flagged sites already destructure `const { error }` and just never reference it, so the fix is largely additive (drop `reportSupabaseError(error, ctx)` on the error path). **EXEMPLAR SHIPPED `434f0979`:** TableChat.send (chat message silently lost + input cleared) -> now reports + preserves input. **PHASING:** Batch 1 = contained content-loss / false-success user-facing sites in small non-hot files (TableChat done; messages send/block, StoryActionBar/stories delete+leave, characters/CharacterCard duplicate, campaign-sheet clock, character-sheet stat, gm-screen scratch autosave, CommunityMoraleModal finalize, savePin). Batch 2 = the combat-hot cluster (`useRollResolution.ts` blast/AoE, table `page.tsx` session/state writes, NpcCard/NpcRoster/CampaignObjects) - LOAD-BEARING, needs care + likely a 2-client verify, do NOT rush. RLS-shaped subset ROUTED TO PUFFER (moderate:266 + stories/new:196 look like genuine bugs, not just observability; rest surface-then-assess). Feed/progression-log wrapper writes (~40, lower severity - missing feed row, no state loss) + telemetry (~15, excluded) are lowest priority. AWAITING XERO's scope/priority call on Batch 1 vs Batch 2 ordering before the bulk pass.
- [x] **[SHIPPED - functional bug, Puffer-routed] /logging visitor search only searched the newest 100 rows.** `app/logging/page.tsx` fetched `visitor_logs .limit(100)` and filtered that capped snapshot client-side, while the tab count showed the true total (11k+). A user whose last logged visit had scrolled past row 100 returned ZERO hits (Xero hit this searching a live "Online Now" user - presence is a separate system, so their last logged visit can be arbitrarily old). Fixed: a debounced, seq-guarded server-side search - resolves `username -> user_id` via `profiles.ilike` first (username isn't a `visitor_logs` column), then `.or()` ilike across page/city/country_code/ip_address OR `user_id.in.(...)`, limit 500. Term sanitized (strip PostgREST-reserved/wildcard chars - grammar safety, not injection; PostgREST parameterizes). Server columns kept in sync with the client haystack so a server match is never hidden by the client re-filter. Include/exclude chips still refine client-side on the returned set. tsc + tests + gates green. **User Events tab: FIXED too (HP, same commit-batch)** - identical debounced/seq-guarded server-side search on event_type + username (user_id.in). One documented limit: metadata is jsonb and PostgREST can't ilike a whole-column text cast, so a term appearing ONLY inside a metadata payload stays window-bounded (client haystack still matches within the returned set).
- [ ] **[NICE-TO-HAVE - Puffer hub SQL, deferred 2026-08-02] /logging search RPC.** A small SQL search function would clean up two things the client-side PostgREST fix can't do well: (a) exclude-negation across columns + cross-table username matching across ALL rows (not just the current window) for chip-only filters, and (b) full metadata (jsonb) search via `metadata::text ILIKE` for the User Events tab. Not urgent - the reported bug (typed search finding users across all 11k rows) is already fixed client-side. Puffer picks this up as a normal hub SQL task whenever convenient; HP wires the UI to the RPC when it lands.

### 🚀 PRE-BETA-500 SCALE (Tier 2, HP handoff 2026-06-23) - triaged
- [x] **[SHIPPED 2026-07-09, Puffer] check-arch seam ratchet regex counts `Array.from(` as a DB read.** Fixed with an uppercase-receiver negative lookbehind `/(?<![A-Z][A-Za-z0-9_$]*)\.from\(/g` - any static call on a constructor/namespace (`Array.from`, `Uint8Array.from`, `SomeClass.from`) is excluded; supabase-shaped receivers (`db().from`, `supabase.from`) still count. 90 false positives eliminated (baseline ratcheted 1028 -> 938 via `--save`); regex cases verified against both shapes. The now-moot "Spread (not Array-dot-from)" workaround comment in CampaignMap.tsx removed.
- [ ] **[ROUTED TO HP, verified 2026-07-09 by Puffer] CampaignMap pin fingerprint hashes the WRONG subset - GM edits to hidden pins churn every player's map.** `components/CampaignMap.tsx` ~650: the change-fingerprint feeds `allPins` (and raw `npcData`) into the hash, but players render only the `visible` (revealed) subset - so a GM editing a still-hidden pin (or a hidden NPC's name) changes the fingerprint on player clients, triggering the destructive marker rebuild the fingerprint exists to prevent (closes open popups, cancels drags). Fix: fingerprint what the client actually renders - `visible` + the post-filter `npcsByPin` content (for GM, visible === allPins so no behavior change). Also while in there: the `[campaignId]`-effect cleanup does not reset `clusterGroupRef`/`pinsFingerprintRef` (latent stale-ref on campaign switch) - reset both in the cleanup. FLAG FOR XERO (judgment call, not a bug): reconcile poll cadence went 10s -> 30s during hardening; worst-case player-stale window now exceeds the 21s incident that motivated 10s.
- [x] **[SHIPPED 2026-07-31 - HP] Broken-weapon Attack gate used a browser `alert()`.** `app/stories/[id]/table/page.tsx` Attack button gated with `alert()` on 4 conditions (no weapon readied / Broken / empty clip / nothing left to throw) - violated the no-browser-dialogs rule (`feedback_no_browser_dialogs`). Fixed: added a reusable transient in-table `combatNotice` toast (amber warning chrome matching the "Aimed" combat chips, auto-dismiss 2.8s, seq-keyed so a repeated identical message still re-fires, click-to-dismiss) and routed all 4 gates through `flashCombatNotice()`. tsc + 934 tests + font/role guardrails green. NOTE: the broader `alert()` sweep in this file (~20 more, mostly DB-error paths + one kick `confirm()`) remains - the toast helper is now in place to migrate them incrementally.
- [x] **[SHIPPED 2026-08-02, HP - all 4 feeds done] shared realtime-reconcile hook for the table surface.** FOUNDATION SHIPPED (`631b234a`): `lib/realtime/usePostgresSubscription` gained an opt-in `reconcile` net (catch-up on subscribe + visibility + 30s poll), additive so existing callers are unchanged. HARDENED 2026-07-09 (pre-adopter, per review): all three reconcile triggers now route through `lib/realtime/reconcileScheduler.ts` - runs are SERIALIZED (never overlap, so a slow stale refetch can't resolve after and clobber a newer one) and burst-COALESCED (5s min gap between starts, one trailing run queued while in flight, disposed on unmount); 7 unit tests in `tests/lib/reconcile-scheduler.test.ts`. Caller refetches should still use the loadSeqRef pattern if they race their own event-handler writes. REMAINING (load-bearing, needs 2-client verify): migrate the hand-rolled subs to it with `reconcile: refetch` - `RollsFeed.tsx:229`, `TableChat.tsx:146` (both bare `.subscribe()`, no catch-up, no poll = can permanently miss a roll/chat msg), `CampaignPins.tsx:178` (has subscribe+visibility, lacks poll -> sidebar lags the polling map), `PlayerNotes.tsx:58`. Then 2-client test each. Verified holes: `RollsFeed.tsx:229` + `TableChat.tsx:146` have bare `.subscribe()` on postgres_changes with NO SUBSCRIBED catch-up, no broadcast belt (no roll_logged/chat_message broadcast exists - checked every `.send()`), no poll: a focused tab that drops one event permanently misses that chat message / out-of-combat roll until refresh. `CampaignPins.tsx:178` has SUBSCRIBED+visibility but no poll, so the sidebar list can stay stale while the map (which now polls) converges - the two views of the same data visibly disagree. Right altitude: extract one shared reconcile hook (SUBSCRIBED catch-up + visibilitychange + fingerprint-guarded slow poll around a refetch fn, per the hardened CampaignMap pattern in lessons.md 2026-07-01) and adopt it in CampaignPins, RollsFeed, TableChat, PlayerNotes. Careful pass + 2-client test, same risk class as T2-4.
  - **[SHIPPED 2026-08-02, HP - RollsFeed + TableChat done]** The "bare .subscribe(), NO catch-up, no poll" description above was STALE: verified both already had subscribe-refetch (`RollsFeed.tsx:228`/`TableChat.tsx:145`) + a `visibilitychange` catch-up (the GrumpyBattersby fix), each with a `refetchSeqRef` stale-drop guard. So the only real remaining hole was a tab left OPEN and FOCUSED dropping a single event (tab-switch was already covered). Closed it by mirroring the shipped CampaignMap pin reconcile poll: a 30s visible-only `setInterval(refetch)` in each component's visibility effect - no reconcileScheduler needed, the existing seq guard handles a low-frequency poll's overlap. tsc + 937 tests green, self-ship. Scale: +30s poll on roll_log + chat_messages per client (~34 req/s total at Beta-500, visible-tabs-only) - same tradeoff class as the pin/vehicle polls.
  - **[SHIPPED 2026-08-02, HP, `ffcb33a9` - CampaignPins + PlayerNotes done, ALL 4 FEEDS COMPLETE]** Same 30s visible-tab-only poll pattern applied to the 2 remaining feeds, approved by Puffer as hub (`tasks/decisions.md` "30s visible-tab-only reconcile poll is the standing pattern"). Bonus catch: `loadPins`/`loadShared` had NO seq guard at all, so their existing multi-trigger reloads (postgres_changes + broadcast + SUBSCRIBED + visibility) already had a latent reload-vs-reload clobber race, independent of the new poll - added a `loadSeqRef` stale-drop guard to both, closing that too. tsc + 937 tests + gates green. Poll-count watch (per the standing decision's ceiling): table surface is now at 6 low-frequency visible-tab-gated pollers (Rolls/Chat/Pins/Notes + CampaignMap pins + vehicles) - still trivial in aggregate, not yet worth consolidating, but the number to keep an eye on before adding a 7th. **Owes 2-client verify for all 4 feeds** - routed to Comms.
- [x] **[SHIPPED `02f61c59` 2026-06-30; hardened `b825087f` 2026-07-01; 2-CLIENT VERIFIED 2026-07-06] pin reveal/hide didn't reach players without a manual refresh.** Verify (GM+player windows): reveal/hide propagates on its own ~sub-30s, open popup stays open across ticks, GM pin-drag sticks, freshly revealed NPC persists in the pin popup - all confirmed by Xero. PtC data fixes (6 pregens in the official picker, Gates order 1,2,3,4) and the shared/GM-only notes split also eyeballed live same pass. Root cause: a still-visible, still-subscribed tab silently misses BOTH the fire-and-forget `pins_changed` broadcast AND the RLS-flaky postgres_changes belt; existing catch-ups only fire on (re)subscribe / tab-visible. Fix: reconcile poll (visibility + map-mounted guarded, 30s post-hardening) in `CampaignMap.tsx` so convergence never depends on catching an ephemeral event; 2026-07-01 review added the latest-ref closure fix, in-flight seq guard, and change-fingerprint skip (no popup-close/drag-cancel churn). Diagnosed from paired GM+player playtest recorder dumps (2026-06-30). Lesson logged (incl. hardened pattern). Sibling components routed above.
- [x] **[T2-1 HIGH - SHIPPED + 2-CLIENT VERIFIED 2026-06-29] campaign-scope the 3 unfiltered postgres_changes subs.** Puffer denormalized `campaign_id` onto `scene_tokens`/`npc_relationships`/`community_members` (backfill + BEFORE-INSERT trigger deriving from parent + publication) last session; HP added the one-line `filter: campaign_id=eq.${id}` to each sub (`07a0f495`). VERIFIED 2026-06-29 via two synchronized recorder dumps (GM+player): token moves + grid/lock + NPC restore all deliver GM->player at 100-600ms, no sub killed by the filter; DB-layer re-confirmed (0 NULL campaign_id, triggers correct, all 3 in publication). `npc_relationships`/`community_members` not write-exercised this run (same construction, high confidence). Detail: `tasks/realtime-2client-verify-testplan-2026-06-29.md`.
- [x] **[SCALE - SHIPPED 2026-08-02, HP] the redundant 3s vehicles poll -> 30s + visible-tabs-only.** `page.tsx` `setInterval(refetchVehicles, 3000)` re-read `campaigns.vehicles` every 3s per client forever (~167 req/s at Beta-500). NOT removed outright: the in-code comment documents that the realtime jsonb-over-vehicles path is FLAKY and the 4 cross-tab paths (storage/BroadcastChannel/focus/poll) exist because "a single one keeps dropping under load" - so the poll is a deliberate popout-sync last-resort, not pure redundancy. Took Puffer's sanctioned `>=30s` alternative + added a `document.visibilityState === 'visible'` gate: ~10x load cut (167->~17 req/s, and backgrounded tabs skip it entirely), net still holds (a re-shown tab re-syncs immediately via the `focus` path). tsc + tests + gates green.

Now count updates immediately; roster resolve is debounced + only fetches uncached ids; roster rebuilt from cache for present ids. `components/Sidebar.tsx`.
- [x] **[T2-3 HIGH - AUDIT MISFIRE, no change by design] cap/paginate "unbounded" reads.** Verified callers: `rollLogForCampaign` (roll-log.ts:65) has ONE caller - the end-session snapshot (page.tsx:3448) - capping it silently truncates saved session logs (data loss); it's a one-shot, not a hot realtime read. `getCampaignNpcs` (campaign-npcs.ts:24) is the shared FULL-set read; RestorePickerModal + many table consumers set `campaignNpcs` (all) and look up dead/inactive NPCs by id - status-filtering the helper breaks them. Both left as-is intentionally; the live-feed read is already bounded (handoff "SOLID - don't touch").

Real win but realtime-merge risk (whisper visibility, redaction, dedup, auto-flip) - needs a careful pass + 2-client test, not a tail-of-session rush.
- [ ] **[T2-5 MED - QUEUED/LOW-PRIORITY, HP] batch campaign-clock drainers.** `lib/campaign-clock.ts` per-row `await update().eq(id)` in drainer loops -> `.in()` reads + batched writes. NOTE on reassessment: loops are party-sized (handful of PCs) and run once per GM time-advance (not a concurrency hot path); multi-row writes need upsert-with-full-row (clobber risk) on an 838-line load-bearing file. Modest benefit / real risk - low priority.

### 🎮 PLAYTEST NOTES 2026-06-23 (recorder marks, session 01-18-24) - SPEC LOCKED, TO SHIP
All decisions confirmed by Xero 2026-06-23. Build order: loot-action -> ping -> pistol-whip -> loot-bullets -> Disarm -> hidden-NPC-fog. Gus parked. Weapons audit separate.
- [x] **[SHIPPED] Loot/Search costs 1 action, COMBAT-ONLY** - searching a container charges the active combatant 1 action, deduped per container per combat (grab everything inside for free), free out of combat. `searchedContainersRef` + `chargeContainerSearch()` wired into both ObjectCard loot paths (onLoot/onSearchEmpty x3 sites). Corpse "Search Remains" already charged; this closes the object/container gap.
- [x] **[SHIPPED] Player PING auto-centers the map to the PING LOCATION for all other viewers.** - new `centerOnCell()` in TacticalMap (mirrors centerViewport via centerScrollOnCell); called from the `gm_ping` broadcast handler. Broadcasts don't echo to sender, so only OTHER viewers recenter - the pinger keeps their view.
- [x] **[SHIPPED] Pistol-whip: melee with an equipped RANGED weapon** - when a PC/NPC has a ranged primary and no carried melee weapon, the Melee button now offers an improvised strike with the gun: `2+1d3` blunt, Melee Combat to-hit. New `forceMelee` flag on `WeaponContext` threads through `computeAttackCmod` (PHY defense not DEX) + the damage calc (`isMelee`, +PHY damage) + the wound-infection guard (blunt = no infection). All changes additive/flag-gated so existing weapons are untouched. TSC + 885 green.
- [x] **[SHIPPED] Loot bullets: generated NPC ranged weapons spawn with `1d6-1` loaded rounds** (0-5, chance of empty) instead of a full clip - the looted weapon inherits it, so picking a gun off a body yields scarce ammo. `lib/npc-generator.ts` (new d6() helper, all 3 weapon-tier sites). TWO judgment calls flagged for Xero: (1) KEPT reloads (d3 spare clips) - so a looted gun is 1d6-1 loaded + spare clips, not pure-scarce; zero reloads if you want total = 1d6-1. (2) Applies to NEWLY-generated NPCs only; existing campaign NPCs keep their full clips.
- [x] **[SHIPPED 2026-06-23, commit `6fdd7ce6`] DISARM combat action.** Built on the grapple engine exactly as planned: `'disarm'` mode, attacker skill picker (Unarmed/Athletics), defender contests `max(Unarmed,Athletics)`, 1 action. On a win the defender's primary weapon drops as a lootable ground token at their map cell (no-map fallback: into their own inventory, never destroyed). DB writes via new `lib/data/combat.ts` `disarmAndDrop` (no inline `.from`). Disarm button next to Grapple; verdict + skill-aware breakdown copy in the result card. **Plus the root-cause fix the spec depended on:** looting a weapon was writing its name to `equipment[]` (a dead legacy list nothing in combat reads) instead of `inventory[]` (what Ready Weapon / Melee / encumbrance read) - so looted weapons could never be Readied. New `lib/loot.ts` `routeLootedItem` routes weapons -> inventory, gear -> equipment; wired into all 4 loot sites (ObjectCard x2, CampaignObjects x2). Also fixes loot-bullets + NPC-corpse loot. 7 new unit tests; 892 green; arch seams hold 1030/22/0. **Live-verify owed:** [tasks/disarm-loot-testplan-2026-06-23.md](disarm-loot-testplan-2026-06-23.md) (2-client: disarm -> ground token -> loot -> Ready -> fire).
- [ ] **[FEATURE] Hidden-NPC fog occlusion** - a token set to SHOW renders for a player ONLY when that player's character can see the cell (not fogged / has line-of-sight); otherwise hidden even with SHOW on. Rides on the per-player vision system (settles the Group/Individual lighting too).
- [ ] **[BUG - PARKED, Xero watching] Gus couldn't ready an inventory gun** - "Equip from Inventory" list (page.tsx:9527) filters `inv.filter(i => getWeaponByName(i.name))`, so a gun whose inventory name doesn't exactly match a catalog weapon never appears; a looted (catalog-named) gun does. Xero will watch for repro detail (custom-typed vs from-catalog name).
- [ ] **[AUDIT - Xero + HP] Revisit the weapons list for completeness + damage consistency** - add missing weapons (e.g. **Revolver**), then analyze damage values across the catalog for internal consistency/balance (`lib/weapons.ts`). Damage values are Xero's canon call.

### 🚨 E2E regressions found 2026-06-11 (ROUTED -> HP) - BOTH RESOLVED
- [x] **[RESOLVED 2026-06-11 `780d452`][HIGH] Clone NPC uses original name after rename** - `npc-roster-crud.spec.ts` un-parked + GREEN. Root cause: `handleClone` in `NpcRoster.tsx` captured `npc.name` and `npcs` from render-time closure; `setNpcs()` from the prior rename hadn't propagated when the clone was triggered. Fix: fetch fresh rows from DB (`npcsForRoster`) at the top of `handleClone`; use `current` (fresh row by ID) for all fields. Stale-closure-on-async-DB-write pattern - now in lessons.md.
- [x] **[RESOLVED 2026-06-11 `383bc26`][MEDIUM] pins-catchup visibilitychange no longer triggers CampaignPins reload** - `pins-catchup.spec.ts` un-parked + GREEN. Root cause: The Arena has active combat; `loadInitiative` -> `setCombatActive(true)` -> `[combatActive,showTacticalMap]` effect auto-switches `gmTab` pins->npcs, unmounting CampaignPins and removing its `visibilitychange` handler AFTER the initial Pins click. Fix (test-side, correct approach): add a second Pins click + 400ms wait AFTER the 1500ms combat-settle; by that point the auto-switch has fired and the re-mount registers a stable handler.

### 🎨 PLAYTEST ROUTES - SESSION 24 (2026-06-16, Test Bed, Xero GM / Tony Bushell / Knox Koss)
- [x] **[FIXED 2026-06-16 `550e252` + 2026-06-17 `6d86456` + 2026-06-18 `fd82cf4`][HIGH] Charge cancel: modal cancel must revert token to origin** - Xero marked this twice: "charge when canceled is broken" (00:51:28 UTC) and "charge failed again - hit charge, hit cancel, still got the CHARGE modal and two moves" (01:01:45 UTC). Fix 1 (`550e252`): route "Cancel Move" button to `handleMapMoveCancel()`. Fix 2 (`6d86456`): defensive guard in `handleMapMoveComplete` - only honour `pendingChargeRef` when `moveMode?.feet === 20`; if the user switched to a 10-foot regular Move, any surviving charge ref is stale and cleared. Dump analysis 2026-06-17 confirmed the bug persisted through fix 1 due to a React state-timing edge case. 877 tests green. Testplan: [tasks/charge-cancel-testplan-2026-06-17.md](charge-cancel-testplan-2026-06-17.md).
- [x] **[FIXED 2026-06-17 `1c88383`][MEDIUM] Stun weapon rebalance + PHY inflation bug** - Real-world analysis: TASER (NMI) > Stun Gun (pain compliance) > Cattle Prod (livestock tool). Existing code already zeroed finalWP for stun weapons; the bug was PHY adding to rawWP before the RP multiplier, inflating RP for Melee stun weapons. Schema: TASER rpPercent 400->600 (6 RP), Cattle Prod damageBase 2->1 / rpPercent 400->200 (2 RP), Stun Gun added (new, Melee/Engaged, 1WP base, rpPercent 400, 4 RP). PHY exclusion fixed in main attack path, stray-shot path (also reordered isStunWeapon before rollDamage), and insight reroll path (which was also missing rpFromRaw and finalWP zeroing entirely). 877 tests green (+2 new). Testplan: [tasks/stun-weapon-rebalance-testplan-2026-06-17.md](stun-weapon-rebalance-testplan-2026-06-17.md). Design decisions made this session: stun weapons cannot kill (WP always zeroed), PHY should not add to stun weapons (electrical output is fixed, not strength-dependent), Cattle Prod = pain only (2 RP fixed).
- [ ] **[ROUTED -> HUNT & PECK 2026-06-16][LOW] Recorder mark prompt: replace `window.prompt()` with in-app input** - Tony's mark "when recruiting there was a browser level warning" (2026-06-16T00:20:57 UTC, ~35s after closing the Recruit modal) is the recorder's own `window.prompt()` dialog, NOT a bug in the Recruit/Apprentice path. Puffer confirmed: the "Take as Apprentice" handler (`app/stories/[id]/table/page.tsx:10181-10215`) is clean (Supabase update + state + progression log + custom event, no native dialogs); `closeRecruitModal` (`page.tsx:4123-4129`) is clean (state resets only). Root cause: `components/PlaytestRecorder.tsx:244` uses `window.prompt('Mark this moment - what happened?')` to capture mark text. A native browser prompt is jarring/unexpected for Thriver playtesters in an SPA context - Tony didn't know what triggered it and filed it as a "warning." Fix: replace the `window.prompt()` call with an in-app floating input (a fixed-position overlay that appears on Ctrl+Shift+M, collects text via an `<input>`, confirms on Enter/button, cancels on Escape). Low priority - recorder is Thriver-only, doesn't affect core table experience. Testplan: [tasks/recorder-mark-prompt-testplan-2026-06-16.md](recorder-mark-prompt-testplan-2026-06-16.md).
- [ ] **[ROUTED -> HUNT & PECK 2026-06-16][LOW] Native `<select>` picker UX friction on three modal surfaces** - Both Xero and Tony repeatedly triggered `button: -1` events (native OS dropdown dismissed without choosing) requiring 2-4 attempts before a selection registered. Affects three surfaces: (1) Grant Advantage skill picker (table page), (2) Recruit skill picker (`components/CommunityProxyRecruitModal.tsx`), (3) Community Add Member character picker (`components/CampaignCommunity.tsx`). On Windows 11 Chrome the native dropdown can close on slight outside-click without committing the selected item. Fix: replace all three `<select>` pickers with a custom dropdown (styled `<ul>` on click, keyboard-accessible, closes only on item selection or explicit Escape). Same fix pattern for all three - worth doing in one pass. Not a table blocker; degrades repeated community-management UX noticeably.

  - **DONE - canon prose** (`ebb19ce`) + **Phase 1 groundwork**: `lib/combat-damage.ts` `applyDamageTransition` (centralized mortal/incap/stress decision) + 10 unit tests (`7c45430`). Verified: normal Unarmed damage already SRD-correct (1d3+PHY+Unarmed); the gap is Subdue-only.
  - **DONE - Phase 2a Subdue** (`f5b4465`): `grappleMode` added to the grapple modal; Subdue is now opposed PHY+Unarmed vs PHY+Unarmed/Athletics, fists-only, deals 1 WP + (1d3+PHY+Unarmed) RP via the new `lib/data/combat` `applyDamageToPc`/`applyDamageToNpc` seams (which funnel `applyDamageTransition` - same canon path as a weapon hit, incap/mortal/Stress + PC & NPC). `setInitiativeActions` seam added to `lib/data/tactical`. executeRoll untouched. **Live-verify owed:** [tasks/subdue-rework-testplan.md](subdue-rework-testplan.md) (grab -> Subdue to RP 0 -> incap + Stress pip, 2 clients).
  - **DONE - Phase 2b Break Free** (`e5c1b61`): opposed escape; breaker spends 1 action, grappler contests reactively (no action); breaker win = grapple ends (`setGrappledBy` null seam); non-win (grappler win OR tie) = breaker +1 RP via `applyDamageToPc/Npc` + stays held. Modal break-free mode = fixed "Held by" grappler target. Closes Xero's worked-example loop (grappled -> escape attempt -> fail/retry). Live-verify in [tasks/subdue-rework-testplan.md](subdue-rework-testplan.md) §7.
  - **DONE - Phase 3** (`054b3d9`): `initiative_order.pending_action_loss boolean DEFAULT false` column applied to live DB. `activateUpdate()` reads it - gives 1 action (not 2) + resets flag. Grapple success: if defender has actions, `consumeAction` as before; if 0 actions, `setInitiativePendingActionLoss(true)` carries to next turn. Then **Phase 4:** modal polish + the deferred `executeRoll` consolidation onto `applyDamageToPc/Npc`.
  - **[FINDING] standalone Subdue (combat-action row 33, button at `:6128`) deals FULL WP, canon says 50% WP** ("non-lethal attack - full RP, 50% WP"). Distinct from the grapple-choke Subdue (Xero confirmed distinct 2026-06-01). The engine has no `wpPercent` so 50%-WP was never implemented. Small separate fix; route when touching combat damage.

Because the GM LOCKED the map (`willStartPan: !mapLocked`, L3142 - confirmed in tony's dump), the player cannot pan to recover -> stranded on empty map. Same family as the "Stansfield's Gas Station" img_scale fix (L845-867) but that fixed *what fraction* players see, not the *center race* after the scale change. **FIX LEAD:** re-run the centroid framing AFTER img_scale settles (frame in a layout effect keyed on the resize / bgLoadTick, or clear `centeredSceneIdRef` when img_scale changes so it re-centers once more). The "center on my token" escape hatch has SHIPPED (`fca10a6` - Center button top-right, visible only when mapLocked && !isGM, calls centerViewport()); the underlying race is still OPEN. **IMMEDIATE PLAYTEST UNBLOCK (operational, told Xero):** GM unlocks the map -> players pan to bottom-right. No deploy needed. Repro campaign cc766e7f, scene "Spring Valley RV & Storage".
- [x] **[CODE SHIPPED 2026-05-27 (2 commits) - 12-check 2-client gate ALL-PASS 2026-05-30 IS the visual eyeball confirmation] img_scale divergence FIXED + fit-to-width display model.** **FOLLOW-UP per Xero's spec (2026-05-27): fit-to-PANEL-WIDTH display.** The whole composite now scales so the grid extent fills the center panel's WIDTH on every machine (taller maps scroll down); `cell_px` sets how many squares subdivide that width (square size), grid still spans full width + grows down to cover the map; each client has a LOCAL zoom slider (100% = fill-width, up to 300% to zoom in) that never affects anyone else (removed the GM-zoom auto-broadcast). One `effectiveScale(containerWidth, gridW, zoom)` helper (lib/tactical-view, 4 tests) drives BOTH draw + pointer<->cell math so they can't drift. Fit-to-Screen now fits the WHOLE map (both dims) + centers. Everyone sees the same map LAYOUT; only absolute size + personal zoom differ. Below: the prior lock-bg-to-grid commit. FIX SHIPPED (lock-bg-to-grid, `components/TacticalMap.tsx`): the background now renders to exactly COVER the grid extent (`grid_cols*cell_px x grid_rows*cell_px`) instead of `naturalWidth*img_scale`, so art + grid + tokens are ONE rigid composite - tokens always on the art, identical for all clients (grid dims + cell_px are shared DB fields). On image load + cell-size change the grid auto-fits the image's aspect (`gridToCoverMap`, GM-only, persisted). The independent img_scale corner-resize handles (the decoupler that made tokens "bounce") are REMOVED, and img_scale is retired from the render path entirely (DB column kept, unused). Fit-to-Screen / Share View are ZOOM-only (never rescale the shared map). All gates green (697 tests, tsc, arch ratchet improved 4298->4246 LOC, font, em-dash); E2E source-guard green (0 setImgScale calls now). **Testplan `tasks/tactical-bg-grid-lock-testplan-2026-05-27.md` (2 browsers).** EXISTING scenes re-fit their grid to the image on next GM load -> a one-time token reposition (off-grid tokens still draw + are grabbable); fresh scenes correct from the start. **REMAINING: Xero's 2-client visual confirmation** (canvas, not automatable). Below is the prior partial-fix history. What 6ef34ce + fca10a6 DID fix: img_scale is now SHARED + authoritative from the DB, per-client auto-fit REMOVED, GM corner-resize persists it, fit-to-screen is per-client ZOOM, locked-map "Center" escape hatch. Scene-open stays "100% centered on party" (Xero declined the 4.3 fit-zoom). **WHAT'S STILL BROKEN (Xero eyeball 2026-05-27, screenshots):** the GM's tactical pane shows the arena art as a small tile top-left while tokens scatter across a huge BLACK grid - the background does NOT fill the grid; and dragging the corner resize handle makes the TOKENS BOUNCE. ROOT (= the spec's 4(c), the one piece never implemented): the background draws at `naturalWidth*imgScale` while grid+tokens draw at `grid_cols*cell_px` - TWO INDEPENDENT SCALES. They align only by coincidence. `6ef34ce` made img_scale *shared* but never *locked it to the grid*, so (a) when `naturalWidth*imgScale != grid_cols*cell_px` the art doesn't fill the grid and tokens sit in black, and (b) resizing the bg (img_scale) moves the art but not the tokens (fixed cell_px) -> "tokens bounce". **REAL FIX (next session, load-bearing TacticalMap.tsx - plan + Xero sign-off first): LOCK the background to the grid.** Render the bg to exactly cover the grid extent (`grid_cols*cell_px` x `grid_rows*cell_px`) so bg + grid + tokens are ONE locked composite; size the map via grid dims / the existing `fitToMap` (gridToCoverMap already matches image aspect at cell_px); retire or repurpose the independent img_scale corner-resize (it's what decouples them). Then: art always fills the grid, tokens always on the art, no bounce, identical for all clients (grid dims are already shared in DB). The E2E smoke (`e2e/tactical-map-render.spec.ts`) is GREEN but only proves the DATA layer (shared/persisted scale) - it canNOT see this misalignment (canvas); that's why "smoke-green" was mis-reported as fixed. LESSON LOGGED. Historical analysis below. PROVEN: active scene `0c2ddae8` has stored `img_scale=1`, `cell_px=25`, grid 57x43 (=1425x1075px), background 2048x1536. Grid+tokens draw at shared `cell_px` (getCellSize L1046 returns cellPx, NO img_scale); background draws at `naturalWidth*imgScale` (L1069). NOTHING in the normal UI writes img_scale to DB - `fitToScreen` (L3597-3609) is `setImgScale` LOCAL ONLY; `fitToMap` only writes grid dims. So DB img_scale stays `1`, and `1` triggers the per-client auto-fit (L870 `img_scale===1` guard, runs for GM AND players) = each client fits the bg to its OWN container width, unsaved -> background slides under the fixed grid by a different amount per screen. `1` is OVERLOADED (= "unset/default" AND "100%"), which is why you can't express "natural, no auto-fit". **FIX:** (a) make img_scale a SHARED authoritative value (persist the GM's fit; render bg at that scale for everyone; use zoom/scroll for per-client viewport fit; never silently per-client rescale); (b) split the overloaded `1` into a real null/unset sentinel vs 100%; (c) ideally lock the image-to-grid relationship so cell_px + img_scale move together and are both shared. This kills BOTH this divergence AND the center-race above. The `/tools/rescale-tactical-scenes` tool already exists (recurring pain signal). **DB-SCALE PATH PROVEN DEAD (2026-05-26 live test):** set scene `0c2ddae8` `img_scale=0.6958` (=1425/2048), GM hard-refreshed -> tokens STILL floated in the black void. The client AUTO-FIT OVERRIDES the DB value even after a full reload (suspected stale-closure: the bg-image effect deps are `[scene?.background_url]` only, L878, so the onload L870 guard reads a stale `scene.img_scale` and re-runs the per-client fit, clobbering the value set by L682). Reverted to `img_scale=1`. **Confirmed: NO DB value reconciles this** - the bg auto-fits to each client's container WIDTH while the grid is fixed `cell_px`; those two only align when `cell_px*grid_cols == containerW`, which differs per client, so it diverges for everyone with a different window size. The grid (1425x1075) is also WIDER/TALLER than the auto-fit bg, so tokens near the right/bottom of the grid spill into black (that's the "floating in black" symptom; PCs were at cols 44-56). **Only two real fixes: (a) CODE - shared authoritative img_scale, bg rendered at that scale for all, grid locked to the image, per-client fit via ZOOM not img_scale, and split the overloaded `1`; (b) drop the background (pure grid is consistent).** **BAND-AID applied live (Xero kept the art):** shifted off-map tokens (grid_x>=38) left 38 cols onto the visible map so they're reachable - `tasks/_work/shift-sv-tokens-onmap.sql`; will recur as play moves right. URGENT for HP - this made a live playtest unplayable. **>> FULL FIX SPEC (Puffer, doc-first): [tasks/tactical-map-render-fix-spec-2026-05-26.md](tactical-map-render-fix-spec-2026-05-26.md) - corrected coordinate model (shared authoritative img_scale + per-client fit via ZOOM not rescale), the overloaded-`1` sentinel fix, locked-map "center on me" escape, migration, + a 2-client test plan. This spec consolidates BOTH this divergence item AND the center-race item above. Read it first.**

The old code used those stale values in `frameViewportOnTokens` -> wrong `maxScrollX/Y` -> player's viewport landed at the wrong vertical position (bottom edge of the map instead of the party token region). Fix: compute expected canvas dims from `Math.max(containerW, grid_cols * cell_px * scale)` (matching draw()'s formula) before calling the centering math. REMAINING: GM sees Mikey bottom-right; the player's viewport is scrolled to a different region so Mikey is OFF-screen, and "if the GM moves Mikey 1 row right, the player can no longer see him." ROOT (verified in code): `TacticalMap.tsx:697-700` scrolls a token into view ONLY when it newly APPEARS (`appeared` = new/newly-visible ids); a token that MOVES (existing id, grid_x/grid_y change) triggers NO player auto-scroll. Per-client pan is independent (the rework removed the GM zoom/scroll auto-broadcast; Share View is manual + one-shot, L760-771). So at rest GM and player see different regions, and moves don't follow. **FIX:** keep the RELEVANT token in the player's frame on MOVE (not just appear) - at minimum the ACTIVE combatant (whose turn it is) + the player's own PC: `scrollCellIntoView` for players when its position changes and it's (about to be) off-screen. This is the render-fix spec's section 4.5 ("never strand a viewer / auto-recenter on token change") - shipped for APPEAR, MISSING for MOVE. Gate (`tactical-map-verify-2client-testplan-2026-05-27.md`) checks #1 (token-on-both-screens) + #3 (player-can-see-own-token) FAIL -> NO-GO until this lands. **TONIGHT workaround (GM action): hit Share View to snap players' scroll to the GM's view after moving tokens.** **>> FULL DESIGN SPEC (Puffer, Xero-approved 2026-05-29): [tasks/tactical-map-viewport-model-2026-05-29.md](tactical-map-viewport-model-2026-05-29.md)** - model = ONE shared map + INDEPENDENT pan/zoom + SMART follow (NOT a mirror). Three changes: (1) scale from the SHARED grid metric, not local panel width (`tactical-view.ts:152 effectiveScale`) -> same-window clients render identical + zoom/window stops diverging; (2) keep pan/zoom local; (3) extend the appear-follow (`697-700`) to fire on MOVE for the active combatant + the viewer's own PC, and make "CENTER" recenter on own/active token. Behavior table + acceptance in the spec. Note: the chrome is UNIFORM (260px feed + 240px asset, role-agnostic) - the "different ratios" came from the per-panel-width scale source, NOT chrome.

- [x] **[SHIPPED `13854c4`] "+ NPC" in the initiative bar now picks from the campaign NPC roster (autocomplete) and LINKS the roster record.** Verified 2026-05-28: InitiativeBar "+ NPC" filters `campaignNpcs` as you type; clicking a match calls `onAddNPC(name, npc.id)` -> `addNPC` -> `buildNpcInitiativeRow` sets `npc_id` + portrait + npc_type and rolls `d1+d2 + acumen + dexterity` (matching combat-start + the reroll lookup); no-match falls back to a one-off (npc_id null, plain 2d6). Unit-tested in `tests/lib/initiative-actions.test.ts`. (Original report below.) Symptom (Xero): typing "ash" in +NPC doesn't autofill "Ash Salazar". ROOT (deeper than autocomplete): `components/InitiativeBar.tsx:423-439` is a plain free-text `<input value={npcName}>` with NO datalist/suggestions; on Add/Enter it calls `onAddNPC(name)` = `addNPC` (`app/stories/[id]/table/page.tsx:2568`), which inserts an `initiative_order` row with `character_name` only, **`npc_id` NULL**, and a plain `rollD6()+rollD6()` (NO init modifier). So an added NPC is a disconnected combatant: no ACU/DEX init mod, no WP/RP/status/portrait link, and the next-round reroll (`L1937`: `rosterNpcs.find(n => n.id === entry.npc_id)?.acumen/dexterity`) finds nothing (npc_id null) so it stays modifier-less every round. **PROPER FIX (root-cause, not just autofill):** make +NPC a picker over `campaignNpcs` (already a prop on InitiativeBar, mirror the existing "+ PC" dropdown at L390-410) - filter as the GM types; selecting a roster NPC passes its id so `addNPC` sets `npc_id` and rolls `2d6 + npc.acumen + npc.dexterity` (matching combat-start + the reroll lookup). Keep a free-text fallback for ad-hoc/unnamed NPCs (npc_id null, plain 2d6 - the current behavior, but as the explicit fallback, not the only path). Pure HP (InitiativeBar + addNPC); no Puffer/DB/schema work.

'#39ff14' : '#ff3a1d'` (red/green/red). Original spec below. Today the tactical ping is a canvas-drawn DOUBLE pulse in ONE role color (GM orange / player green): `components/TacticalMap.tsx` draw block L2150-2181, spawned with `count: 2` at L819 (broadcast `gm_ping` handler) and L3059 (local press-hold drop); the loop decrements count (L2174) and re-pulses. The CAMPAIGN ping (the target, `components/CampaignMap.tsx` `dropPing` L559-585 + `cm-ping-pulse` keyframes L294-305) is THREE staggered rings, colors **red `#ff3a1d` -> green `#39ff14` -> red `#ff3a1d`** (role color deliberately ignored - alternating hue is easier to catch peripherally), 0.6s each, 0/400/800ms stagger (~1.4s total). **CHANGE:** (1) `count: 2` -> `count: 3` at L819 + L3059; (2) in the draw block, replace the single `ping.color` stroke/fill with a per-pulse color keyed off `ping.count` (3->`#ff3a1d`, 2->`#39ff14`, 1->`#ff3a1d`) so it cycles red/green/red like the campaign map. - [ ] **[PUFFER - pin realtime propagation; TWO corroborating reports].** (00-15 #2) "i shared a PIN and it didn't show up withoiut a refresh" + (02-22 #4) "pin didnt' show to players as they neared it". Both = a pin that SHOULD be visible to players didn't appear without a manual refresh. FINDINGS (puffer, verified in code): campaign pins are `campaign_pins` (the Leaflet map; `map_pins` = tactical); BOTH are in `sql/_baseline/publication.sql` (L25, L33). Campaign pins do NOT rely on postgres_changes (code says it was "laggy") - they propagate via a **broadcast `pins_changed`**: sent in `components/CampaignPins.tsx:163` on a pin change, received at `CampaignPins.tsx:169` + `CampaignMap.tsx:913` -> `loadPins()`. So the break is in the reveal->broadcast->reload->RLS chain. **PUFFER INVESTIGATION DONE 2026-05-26 - root cause found, fix is HP's lane:**
  - WIRING IS CORRECT (ruled out config/SQL): `toggleReveal`/`revealAll`/`setFolderRevealed` (CampaignPins.tsx:227-241) all call `broadcastPinsChanged()` -> `send({event:'pins_changed'})` on channel `campaign_pins_<id>` (L162-168). The player's `CampaignMap` (L912-915) AND `CampaignPins` (L168-169) BOTH subscribe to that exact channel + `loadPins()` on the event. Channel names match; `campaign_pins` is in the publication; RLS allows member reads of `revealed=true` pins (players query `.eq('revealed', true)`, L112). So NOT a channel-mismatch / RLS / publication / send-missing bug.
  - **ROOT CAUSE: the broadcast is fire-and-forget with NO delivery guarantee.** A player who was disconnected (cf. the "Juno bounced" note above), subscribed late, or just dropped the packet never receives the one-shot `pins_changed` -> never reloads -> pin invisible until a MANUAL REFRESH (which re-runs `loadPins`). The `postgres_changes` "belt" listener is documented-flaky on RLS'd tables (lessons 2026-04-11), so it doesn't reliably cover the gap.
  - **[SHIPPED 2026-05-27 - all 5 surfaces] FIX (components/ = their lane):** added CATCH-UP RELOADS so convergence no longer depends on catching one ephemeral broadcast - each sub now reloads on its channel's `SUBSCRIBED` (re)connect status AND on `document` `visibilitychange` (tab return-to-visible), mirroring the `RollsFeed`/`TableChat` pattern. Done in `CampaignPins.tsx` (loadPins), `CampaignMap.tsx` (loadPins), `PlayerNotes.tsx` (loadShared), `app/npc-sheet/page.tsx` (new `refetchNpc` re-pulls the row), `app/campaign-sheet/page.tsx` (new `catchUp` re-pulls clock+vehicles+scheduleRefetch). Pure additive wiring; no schema/data; broadcast + postgres_changes paths unchanged. Testplan `tasks/pin-catchup-reload-testplan-2026-05-27.md` (needs 2 browsers; verify on live). NOTE -> E2E lane: a reconnect/visibility catch-up assertion would lock this in (not Vitest-testable - channel-lifecycle integration). Low-risk; covers the bounce / late-subscribe / dropped-packet cases. **SWEEP RESULT (Puffer 2026-05-26): the catch-up pattern ALREADY EXISTS in the app - `RollsFeed.tsx:246-249` and `TableChat.tsx:165-168` both `refetch()` on `visibilitychange`; `table/page.tsx:1388/1447` does SUBSCRIBED + visibility. The PINS subs are the OUTLIER that missed it (`CampaignPins.tsx:166-176` + `CampaignMap.tsx:912-915` reload ONLY on the broadcast). So HP's fix = COPY the existing `RollsFeed` pattern onto the two pin subs - not a new mechanism. SECONDARY (AUDIT DONE 2026-05-27 - all 3 CONFIRMED missing the catch-up): `PlayerNotes.tsx` (1 broadcast sub / 0 catch-up), `app/npc-sheet/page.tsx` (1 / 0), `app/campaign-sheet/page.tsx` (2 / 0). So it's a CLASS of 5 surfaces (CampaignMap + CampaignPins + these 3). HP: apply the same `RollsFeed`/`TableChat` visibility+SUBSCRIBED catch-up to all 5 in one batch. Stakes order: pins highest (players rely on revealed locations); campaign-sheet medium (clock/heal/pending-effects stale till refresh); npc-sheet + PlayerNotes lower (GM/owner convenience).**
  - **[E2E REGRESSION SHIPPED 2026-05-27 - locks in the highest-stakes surface]** `e2e/pins-catchup.spec.ts` (green on prod). Targets `CampaignPins` because it's the one BROADCAST-ONLY sub (no postgres_changes), so a REST pin insert fires no app broadcast and reaches it through zero realtime path - isolating the catch-up. Flow: player loads the pin list -> GM REST-inserts a revealed `[E2E]` pin (MISSED) -> negative control asserts it stays absent (no delivery path) -> fire `visibilitychange` (tab-return) -> assert the pin converges in the CampaignPins sidebar (its own list state; CampaignMap can't populate it + renders the name only as a `title=` attr / closed popup, not `getByText`-matchable). Reverting the catch-up wiring => the pin never converges => red. Reversible (pin deleted in teardown). NOTE: the other 4 surfaces (CampaignMap/PlayerNotes/npc-sheet/campaign-sheet) all carry a postgres_changes belt too, so they're not cleanly isolatable the same way; this spec proves the catch-up MECHANISM on the surface that most needed it.
  - **"pin didn't show as they neared it" -> PROXIMITY AUTO-REVEAL IS NOT A FEATURE.** No proximity-reveal code exists; pins reveal only via manual GM toggle. So that note is EITHER the same delivery gap (GM manually revealed as players approached) OR a feature request for auto-reveal-on-proximity. **[XERO DECISION - feature roadmap]:** is proximity auto-reveal wanted? Non-trivial (player position vs pin distance + a reveal trigger + which coordinate system). Default assumption: it's the delivery gap, fixed by the catch-up reload above.

- [x] **FIXED 2026-05-25 (E2E lane, cross-lane, Xero-authorized) - PC-to-PC item trade DESTROYED the item (data loss).** Found building inventory-trade (#13). A Survivor giving an item to another player's PC: the give handler (`app/stories/[id]/table/page.tsx:6930`) writes the RECEIVER's `characters` row from the GIVER's session, but `characters` UPDATE RLS is own-row-only (schema.sql:2187) + Thriver bypass (:2189) - a Survivor giver is neither, so the write matches 0 rows SILENTLY (PostgREST 200 + `[]`). Meanwhile `confirmGive` (InventoryPanel.tsx:168-179) DOES remove the item from the giver's own row (RLS-allowed). Net: sender loses it, receiver never gets it = item destroyed; the `notify_inventory_received` SECURITY DEFINER RPC even tells the receiver they got it. GM-initiated gives work (Thriver bypass), which is why it slipped through. **CONFIRMED on live prod** via reversible REST probe (marv->percy PATCH = 200 + `[]` rows, percy inventory unchanged). Same direct-write pattern in onGiveItemToNpc/Community/Vehicle (6947+) - audit those too. FIX (bright line, not E2E's): a SECURITY DEFINER `give_item_pc_to_pc()` RPC doing both sides atomically + campaign-membership check (mirror `sql/loot-npc-item-rpc.sql` / the vehicle RPC), swapped into the client handler. Full finding: [tasks/finding-pc-trade-rls-dataloss-2026-05-24.md](finding-pc-trade-rls-dataloss-2026-05-24.md). **FIX SHIPPED 2026-05-25:** `onGiveItem` (table/page.tsx) now calls the live `give_item_to_character` SECURITY DEFINER RPC (atomic both-sides) + surfaces errors + refreshes the giver via `loadEntries` (the sender doesn't self-receive the broadcast); `InventoryPanel.confirmGive` skips the client giver-decrement for the PC case ONLY (avoids a double-spend race vs the RPC's `SELECT FOR UPDATE`). The `inventory-trade.spec` PC-trade test is UN-fixme'd + green (item MOVES: receiver gains, giver loses). Full re-cert clean (126 passed; the lone fail was the pre-existing section-c flake, unrelated). NPC/Community/Vehicle gives untouched (their targets are GM-owned campaign tables - separate audit, still queued).

'approved' : 'pending'`) with NO BEFORE INSERT trigger. The campfire equivalent (forum_threads/war_stories/lfg_posts) WAS hardened with `enforce_moderation_on_insert` (sql/moderation-enforce-trigger-2026-05-17.sql, Y3 pre-launch audit) precisely because client-only is bypassable. `map_pins` was left out. Risk Register flipped RED -> GREEN; finding doc marked RESOLVED. Revert if ever needed: `DROP TRIGGER trg_enforce_map_pin_moderation ON public.map_pins`.

Reversible - the single inserted row is deleted in teardown; nothing pre-existing touched. 3 consecutive prod passes confirm determinism. NOT a reveal regression (the feature always worked - the test asserted the wrong thing).

### Architecture review 2026-05-24 (puffer-fish, north-star) - the 5 designed-not-retrofitted moves
Full doc: [tasks/architecture-review-2026-05-24.md](architecture-review-2026-05-24.md). The two missing layers cause nearly every structural pain. **EXECUTABLE PATH (sequenced/gated/de-risked): [tasks/architecture-path.md](architecture-path.md) - PROPOSAL awaiting Xero sign-off (destination read + Stage C tech call).** Priority moves (none emergencies; do #1 after Phase 7 closes, with a design doc + eventually a human architect for the big migration):

Kills the recurring bug class this session kept hitting. **DESIGN DONE 2026-05-24: [tasks/stage-b-conditions-design.md](stage-b-conditions-design.md)** (verified audit + locked engineering calls). Principle: unify the CONTRACT (apply/clear/read/reset via a new `lib/conditions.ts`), NOT the clock (infection=day, MW/incap=round legitimately differ). Un-gated/shipping: type `liveState` (DONE `028a6fa` - ~16 `as any` removed across page.tsx/PlayerStatusChips/CharacterCard/useRollResolution; was the `} as any` at page.tsx:1025, not the schema) -> NEXT: `lib/conditions.ts` API + route the ~9 inlined writes + Restore through it -> char-sheet infection-blind select fix. **2 flags for Xero:** (1) CANON - does the game want NPC stress? (`campaign_npcs` has no stress column). (2) schema-gated - move PC lasting wounds from `characters.data` to a real column for NPC parity + realtime (dry-run -> confirm).
- [ ] **[MEDIUM] Infra-as-code (Stage A1)** - publication membership + RLS + triggers + the 15 orphan-table schemas into versioned CI-applied migrations (closes the silent-config-bug class; the publication gap that cost an hour today). **SCOPED 2026-05-23: [tasks/stage-a-infra-as-code-scope.md](stage-a-infra-as-code-scope.md).** Live inventory confirmed (69 tables / 286 RLS policies / 62 triggers / 72 fns / 21 published; 0 RLS-disabled). The 15 orphans are the CORE tables (campaigns/characters/character_states/profiles/roll_log) - they predate the sql/ habit, so capture must be machine-generated. Approach: `supabase db dump --linked` schema baseline + a publication drift-detector + the discipline rule. **TIER 1 PARTIALLY BUILT 2026-05-23:** publication baseline (`sql/_baseline/publication.sql`, 21 tables) + drift-detector (`scripts/check-publication-drift.mjs`, `npm run check:publication`, tested green + negative-tested) + discipline rule in AGENTS.md. **SCHEMA BASELINE DONE 2026-05-23 (no-Docker route):** Docker not installed, so captured via the `db query --linked` API + Postgres DDL generators - `scripts/capture-schema.mjs` -> `sql/_baseline/schema.sql` (69 tables incl. all 15 orphans, 292 constraints, 121 indexes, 286 policies, 56 triggers, 72 fns; functions/triggers/constraints/indexes exact, tables+policies reconstructed + spot-checked). Re-runnable to refresh. **Tier 1 COMPLETE.** Remaining: Tier 3 (CI drift-check, needs a read-only DB secret) - deferred, not blocking.

ADD TABLE characters` - cheapest to ship, but loads a broad/higher-write definition table into the global realtime fan-out (scale cost grows with write volume, not with the few clients watching). **(B)** remove the dead handler (char-sheet:83-85) - behavior-preserving since it never fired; accept that definition edits don't live-refresh an open sheet. **(C)** replace it with a targeted `character_updated` broadcast on the existing seam (`broadcastOnce`) - precise, no WAL fan-out, matches the re-arch "designed not retrofitted" pattern. Puffer-fish recommendation: **C** (or B if the live-refresh isn't worth the work) over A - don't publish the busiest definition table for a narrow popout nicety. UX-vs-scale call = Xero's.
